we Suns Parallax is an Angle at his Center ſubtended by the Garths Semi-

znimadverfions, and ſo diminiſhe it to 1/,

Yeaus in the Sun, with her apparent diameter ar that time, and other good ar- 
ouments, he determines the Sun's Horizontal Parallax 15” and no more;which 
ſmall quantity, agreeing well with the moſt diligently obſerved Semidiameters 
of all the Planets, and being farther confirmed by all our beſt Teleſcope-ob- 
ſervations of the Moon's Dichotomies and otherwiſe, we accept of, as neareſt 
the truth, and ſufficiently <xaQR. |
